  CanvasMenuItems.cs            # Static: the view sections' canvas-only items, each built from the state the surface hands it (data-block form and position reset, nav route, measure, taxi-route mode radio, hide datablock, Draw route, and the leader-direction / J-ring / cone / blank / unblank overlays, which send through the host), plus Display, which assembles a view's blocks into the submenu; never catalog entries, no MenuIds, never on a quick-command list
  IMenuAircraft.cs              # Read-only view of an aircraft (AircraftType, TrueTrack, IsManeuvering and PatternPosition feed the traffic-advisory matcher's candidates) the applicability predicates, the heading and Warp seed (MagneticHeadingDegrees, the datablock's heading; HeadingDegrees is the true one), the header's state line (CurrentTaxiway, ParkingSpot, GroundAirportId among its reads) the Track submenu (OwnerSectorCode, HandoffPeer, PointoutStatus) the Exit left / right flyouts (ExitsAhead) and the Give way to… rows' meet points (MeetPoints) read, and the deferred reports REPORT armed (ArmedReports: the pattern legs, the N-mile final and the fix, null when none); AircraftModel implements it
  PhaseDisplayNames.cs          # Static For(rawPhase): a raw sim phase's menu display name, the state line's phase segment; a table for the phases whose sim name does not read as English (FinalApproach → Final, InitialClimb → Departure), else CamelCase split to sentence case with acronyms and dynamic tails kept (Holding Short RWY 28R → Holding short RWY 28R)
  IMenuHost.cs                  # What a catalog entry's builder needs from the view that opened the menu, the same on every view: Session, SendAsync, the popups (ShowInputPopup, the command and note flyouts), OpenFlightPlanEditor, BuildFavorites, BuildRpoItems, AssumeSelectedLiveTrafficAsync, the reads (SuggestFixes (the fix suggestions for typed text, as the command bar offers them), GetFieldElevation, GetMva (the MVA sector and floor at a position), GetGroundTrafficRows (every other ground aircraft but delayed spawns as MenuGroundTrafficRows, nearest first), IsOnTaxiRoute, HighlightAircraft (the hover highlight on every ground and radar view), SetScopePreview (a ScopePreview on every ground view, null clears), SetRadarCue (a RadarCue on every radar view, null clears), GetRemainingTaxiRoute (the route an aircraft has left, null with none), GetAirborneTraffic (every other airborne aircraft, nearest first, as MenuAirborneTrafficRows)), the solo advisory flyout's reads and send (SendForResultAsync: SendAsync that hands back the sim's CommandResultDto, null when the command never reached the sim; GetAdvisoryCandidates: every aircraft the client holds, the commanded one included, as the matcher's TrafficAdvisoryCandidates; ObserveAircraftUpdates: calls back on the UI thread once per burst of aircraft updates until the handle is disposed, backed by MainViewModel.AircraftModelUpdated; GetFixPosition; GetReportingPointsNear: the fixes within the matcher's landmark radius of a position, nearest first, up to SoloAdvisoryModel.MaxReportingPoints; GetReportingPointsNearby: the visual reporting points REPORT takes within 10 nm of a position as NearbyReportingPoints, nearest first with distance and true bearing), the ground-movement answers (GetActiveArrivalEnds (the room's active arrival runway ends at an airport, in the room's order), IsToweredAirport (whether the room gives an airport an operating tower), GetPatternRunwayChoices (the pattern entries' runway flyout rows for an airport and physical type, built by PatternRunwayChoices.For from the room's arrival or both-way active runway ends, cached per host so a menu build parses and warns once), GetHoldShortChoices, SetRoutePreview, GetPushbackFaceChoices, GetPushbackTargets, GetPresetTaxiChoices, GetTaxiToRunwayChoices), EnterDrawRoute and EnterPushRoute (which show the primary ground view first), and the point menu's DescribePoint, GetTaxiChoices, GetRunwayHoldShortTargets, GetCustomTaxiSeed and the title's place names (CanTugReach(callsign, node): whether the tug planner finds a move there (gates Push to and Push route…); GetNodeTaxiwayNames(GroundNodeDto): the taxiways meeting at a node, each named once (a fillet arc's names split, ramp and runway names left out); GetHoldShortTaxiwayName(GroundNodeDto): the taxiway a hold short sits on; GetPositions: the room's controller list and the student's TCP as MenuPositions, which the coordination flyouts read; ScratchpadMaxLength: the most characters the room's STARS scratchpads hold, which the Scratchpad box refuses text past); ClientMenuHost serves every member on every view and none throws; no canvas state: each view builds its canvas items itself (CanvasMenuItems)
  InlineBoxRow.cs               # InlineBoxRow (an always-open box row, a plain TextBox or a fix AutoCompleteBox (Input, Text), with its verb drawn inside it, the command column following the text, "can't send" in red (✕ on a CreateSendingAll row) with a reason line for refused text, and the note line under the box; Create(spec, send) is a flyout's last row, whose Item stays open while it cannot send; CreateSendingAll(spec, wholeCommand, send) is one field of a larger command, whose Enter sends the whole command and whose click only focuses the box; CreateFocusingOnClick(spec, send) sends its own command on Enter and only focuses on click, reading "can't send" when refused; PutBesideBox puts controls on the box's line, the Report when… mile chips), InlineBoxSpec (label, glyph, BlankGlyphSlot, verb, placeholder, box name and width, Seed (the text the box opens holding, selected whenever it takes the focus still holding it), FixSuggestions, Read) and InlineBoxReading (Sends / Reads (green note) / Keeps (amber, a present value sent in a blank box's place) / LeavesOut (the column shows —) / Refuses / Blank): the Squawk code box (SQ), the Scratchpad box (SP) and the Say Custom box (SAY)
  MenuPushTarget.cs             # Record Kind (Taxilane | Taxiway | Spot) / Name / Note / PathLengthFt / Command / Facings (MenuPushFacing: Cardinal, Command) / BlockedBy (+ ForcedCommand, the PUSHF form; PushVerb): one Push back to… target, planned from the aircraft's stand or held pose and live-checked against the aircraft about it
  PushTargetList.cs             # The Push back to… targets of one aircraft, which may still be filling: Targets (empty and unsettled while the plan computes), Settled; Pending(seed) is filled by the host's background live plan through Apply, which settles the list and then raises Changed on the UI thread; Ready is settled from the start
  PushbackToMenu.cs             # Static Build: the Push back to… menu over a PushTargetList, one builder for the All Commands submenu and the strip flyout (which follows the list while open through IStripFlyoutContent): a Behind the aircraft section (badge TL/TW) then a Taxi spots section, each by path length with a ~N ft hint; a target with 2+ facings gets `then face` and a chip per cardinal; a blocked row is faded, inert, names the blocker and carries a push anyway chip (ForcedCommand); Computing targets…, Refining targets… (RefiningText) and No push targets from here placeholders; refills in place while open
  MenuCommandChoice.cs          # Record Label/Command?/Preview/Children: a host-answered submenu choice carrying its finished command text and optional preview TaxiRoute (pushback faces, preset taxi routes, the point menu's taxi tree); children make a submenu, no command and no children a disabled row, Separator a separator
  HoldShortChoice.cs            # Records HoldShortChoice (Label, Command, Preview, BarPoint: one Hold short of… stop, a bar along the route), HoldShortRowLabel (Badge TW/RW, Name, Where, DistanceFt rounded to 50 ft), HoldShortDestination (Name "Spot 5", IsRunway, DistanceFt: where the route ends) and HoldShortMenu (RouteLine "route S T V W4 · RWY 30" or "route B W · spot 5", MeLine "now, taxiing on D", Rows nearest first, Destination; Empty), which IMenuHost.GetHoldShortChoices answers
  HoldShortLadder.cs            # Static Build: the Hold short of… route ladder (strip flyout and All Commands) over IMenuHost.GetHoldShortChoices, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout: title row, disabled me row, stop rows (HoldShortBarMark runway/taxiway marks, TW/RW badge, name, where, ~N ft, HS command; HoldShortStopRowTemplate), the dimmed unclickable destination row (HoldShortEndRowTemplate); hidden with no stop. Live refill once a second in the strip flyout (FrozenStops holds the order with the pointer inside, keyed by the HS text; gone rows, appended newcomers, keyboard skips gone rows); a stop's hover or focus drives a ScopePreview (bar box, route to the bar, no aircraft ring)
  TaxiRouteRow.cs               # Records TaxiRouteRow (Badge, Name, Reason, AvailableFt, IsHighlighted, Via, DistanceFt, Command, Preview route, Variants: one Preset taxi route row or one runway entry point of Taxi to runway), TaxiToRunwayMenu (RunwayEnds; Empty), TaxiToRunwayEnd (Designator, Runway, Role Assigned/Departure/Other, State NotSearched/Searching/Ready/Empty, Rows) and TaxiToRunwayEndList (every runway end of the airport in order; SearchRunwayOf searches an unsearched runway in the background; a search function that throws is logged and read as no result)
  AirTaxiFlyout.cs              # Enum AirTaxiVerb (AirTaxi | Land) and static Build: the helicopter Air taxi and Land flyout (strip flyout and All Commands, one builder, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout) over IMenuHost.GetAirTaxiChoices: a filter box (and Land's Keep NODEL box) over a FlyoutTabStrip (Pads · parking, Spots, Runways for Air taxi only), rows nearest first with runway-bar chips, refilled once a second on FlyoutLiveTick; Land prefixes APT when its airport is not the filed destination
  AirTaxiMenu.cs                # Records AirTaxiMenu (AirportId + Pads/Parking/Spots/Runways), AirTaxiDestination (Name, Kind, DistanceFt, Token, BarTaxiway, Chips, IsHeldAt) and AirTaxiChip, enum AirTaxiDestinationKind: the host's answer for the Air taxi / Land flyout
  PointTaxiFlyout.cs            # Static BuildTaxiHere and BuildTaxiToRunway: the ground point menu's taxi flyouts, one builder each for the strip button and its text row, built once per right-click and never refilled. Taxi here over IMenuHost.GetTaxiChoices: several routes open a flyout of two-line rows (route label over its whole TAXI command, distance at the right), a route with variants is a title over its variants, one route with one command sends at once, and no route is a disabled row the strip shows as a faded, inert button (`Taxi here — No route found`). Taxi to runway at a runway-surface click is one entry whose flyout has a tab per runway end under the click, each tab the host's hold-short targets as TaxiToRunwayFlyout title lines over their variants (a target with no route is a dimmed title over a disabled `No route found`); Up/Down/Tab walk the rows, Left/Right the tabs, and the route preview clears on tab change and close
  TaxiToRunwayFlyout.cs         # Static BuildTaxiToRunway: the Taxi to runway flyout (strip flyout and All Commands, one builder, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout) over TaxiToRunwayMenu.RunwayEnds: a FlyoutTabStrip with one tab per runway end (TabLabel: `30 assigned`, `28L departure`, bare designator) above a scrolling rows panel (RowsMaxHeight 420 px) of per-entry title lines (TitleText), two-line MenuTwoLineRow variants and the end's presets; the host is asked once at open, an unsearched tab starts its runway's search when chosen (SearchingText, NoRunwayEntryText, tab state Searching/Empty, a failed search retried on the next choice), Up/Down/Tab walk rows, Left/Right change tab without wrap, route preview per row; nothing refills while open
  ExitFlyout.cs                 # Static Build: the Exit left / Exit right flyouts (strip flyout and All Commands, one builder, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout) over IMenuAircraft.ExitsAhead: a turn-off glyph row per exit, Pilot's choice, on final the "Exit at" box ranked over IMenuHost.GetRunwayExits, dropped rows greyed as "can't make" / "passed" while the flyout is open
  TaxiRouteFlyout.cs            # Static BuildPreset: the Preset taxi route flyout (strip flyout and All Commands) over IMenuHost.GetPresetTaxiChoices, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout; flat two-line route rows nearest first, refilled once a second in the strip with the order frozen while the pointer is inside (FrozenPresets, gone rows, newcomers at the bottom), route preview per row; null with no preset
  MenuTextSeed.cs               # Record Text/Caret: an input popup's initial text and caret (Custom taxi's prefill from the clicked node, naming the clicked runway end on a threshold click)
  MenuCatalog.cs                # Static: one MenuCatalogEntry per MenuIds action (All, Get); a leaf's click sends its command text through IMenuHost.SendAsync; Exit left / Exit right are ExitFlyout.Build entries; Warp… (airborne controllable aircraft only) and Warp here at a map point are WarpFlyout.Build entries, Warp here at a taxi node a WARPG leaf; Command…, Note…, Edit flight plan, Draw taxi route… and Push route… call the host instead; the point.* entries are the point menu's items, and the internal PointFlyHeading(aircraft, point) is the heading its Fly heading sends, which the radar point menu's turn row reads too; `BuildSend` is the one send-item builder, which CanvasMenuItems' overlay items reuse; a pattern entry is a submenu from BuildPatternRunwayFlyout, one MenuGlyphRow per runway end from IMenuHost.GetPatternRunwayChoices, focused on the assigned runway's row; Cleared visual approach is BuildClearedVisual (the visual-only list of ClearedVisualSpec, IFR aircraft only) and Join STAR is BuildJoinStar (JoinStarFlyout)
  ApproachFlyout.cs             # Static Build(label, ApproachVerbSpec, ...) and the panel: the flyout of each of the eight approach verbs and of Cleared visual approach (strip flyout and All Commands, registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout; MenuCatalog routes every approach entry here, Cleared visual with IsVisualList set) over ApproachFlyoutModel: a title line (the verb's label, the force mark for the force pair, Cleared approach's Force toggle), a FlyoutTabStrip of runway tabs and Circling, the pinned default row (focused on open), Visual rows (Cleared and Expect only), the Approach box, the Cancel IFR inline confirmation (Cleared and Expect); the visual list has no tabs, default row or box: the landing runways' visual rows, then a `Non-arrival runways…` fold row over the other ends; reopening resets Force, the box, the tab and the fold
  ApproachFlyoutModel.cs        # ApproachVisualRows, ApproachVerbSpec (label, verb, forced, visual rows, Force toggle verb, Cancel IFR, glyph id, default-row label), ApproachRow and ApproachFlyoutModel (Build, BuildVisualList, Pinned, Rows, HeadRows, OtherRows, BoxCommand): the flyout's data without UI. The visual list (IsVisualList) is one `Visual RWY n` row per runway end (RunwaysFor says which are landing ends), landing ends first, no tabs. Tabs in the room's arrival-runway order (IMenuHost.GetActiveArrivalEnds) then the rest, `{rwy} {count}` labels, the default row, Visual and untowered-airport rows (IMenuHost.IsToweredAirport), `short` and `field not in sight` notes, Force (CVAF / CAPPF), Cancel IFR (CIFR) below FL180 for IFR
  ApproachChoices.cs            # Static: the destination's CIFP approaches as ApproachFlyoutModel reads them (Choices: ordered ILS, LOC, RNAV, RNP, then the rest; Expected, AssignedRunwayApproach, SameRunway); names the kind from the ARINC type code or a circling id's prefix (DescribeCirclingApproach) and holds the ApproachChoice record (short and published names, an RNP approach named RNAV in a default row)
  TakeoffFlyout.cs              # The Cleared for takeoff flyout's one builder (strip flyout and All Commands › Runway): the Initial altitude box (TakeoffAltitudeBox, pre-filled from IMenuAircraft.FiledVfrCruiseFeet), the VFR and IFR rows, each showing the command it sends (TakeoffRowInput None/Heading/Fix/Custom boxes; fix suggestions through IMenuHost.SuggestFixes), and the always-open Custom row last (CustomItem: CTO fixed before the box, blank sends CTO), "···" and faded while a box is empty or unreadable
  AltitudeFlyout.cs             # The altitude flyouts: BuildMaintain (strip flyout and All Commands › Vectors › Altitude › Maintain altitude: MaintainAltitudePanel with the title line, the Altitude box, the 300 px list of rows with the MVA line, MaintainList computing the marks, verbs and MVA greying from an AltitudeReading, NearestIndex) and BuildBox (All Commands › Track and coordination › Data block › Temporary altitude… / Cruise…: AltitudeBoxPanel over an AltitudeBoxKind, previewing TEMPALT / CRUISE or Not a valid altitude); both registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout, built on first open and refreshed once a second on FlyoutLiveTick
  HeadingFlyout.cs              # The Fly heading flyout's one builder (strip flyout and All Commands › Vectors › Heading: Fly heading, Turn left / right and the two degree entries, each a HeadingFlyoutPreset of label, HeadingVerb and HeadingFlyoutFocus; registered through QuickCommandStrip.FollowInFlyout): HeadingFlyoutPanel (title, Present heading FPH row, FH/TL/TR toggle, Heading box with BoxCommand L180/R090, the HeadingRose (HeadingRose.cs) between the LT/RT degree-chip columns, readout), the pure helpers (Command, HeadingAt, RoundedToFive, Turn, TurnText) and the once-a-second follow of the aircraft on FlyoutLiveTick
  HeadingRose.cs                # The north-up compass rose the Fly heading and Join radial flyouts draw: rim, ticks, needle at the present heading, the pick with its inner turn arc (ShowTurnArc off for Join radial), CentreCaption (the navaid in Join radial's centre); press or drag picks and the release sends (Chosen), StepBy for the panel's keys
  SpeedFlyout.cs                # The speed flyout's one builder (Build: strip Assign speed and All Commands › Vectors › Speed › Assign speed / Speed…, registered through PanelFlyoutContent.Attach): SpeedFlyoutPanel (title with now IAS and Mach, Speed box with its readout, Resume normal speed RNS and Final approach speed RFAS rows, the knots chips eight to a row, the Mach chips at or above FL240, chips filled for now and outlined for assigned and typed, the arrow-key walk) over MenuCatalog.SpeedValues, KnotsCommand / MachCommand, PresentKnots / PresentMach
  AirborneTrafficFlyout.cs      # Static BuildReportInSight / BuildFollow: the Report traffic in sight… (RTIS) and Follow traffic… (FOLLOW, FOLLOWF) flyouts on one layout (strip flyout and All Commands; in solo training Report traffic in sight… attaches an AdvisoryFlyoutContent instead): title, Force toggle, Traffic box, the nearest airborne aircraft over IMenuHost.GetAirborneTraffic, 1 s refill with held order and gone rows, radar cue through RadarCueTracker, inert when nothing is airborne
  AirborneTrafficRows.cs        # Static: Row (a MenuAirborneTrafficRow from two aircraft), ListedCount 8 / ListedRadiusNm 10, Matches (callsign or type filter), the list with the in-sight lead on top, DirectionWord, LegWord, IsInSight, IsFollowing
  AdvisoryFlyoutContent.cs      # The internal IStripFlyoutContent that attaches an advisory panel (IAdvisoryFlyoutPanel: the solo SoloAdvisoryPanel or the FieldAdvisoryPanel) on the strip's flyout or the All Commands submenu: refilled on open and once a second while the strip's flyout is open, follows IMenuHost.ObserveAircraftUpdates while open; a send the sim takes closes the flyout and the menu, a refusal keeps it open. AdvisorySend holds the in-flight guard and the Refused / Not sent / Set the … first notes both flyouts share
  SoloAdvisoryFlyoutHost.cs     # SoloAdvisoryFlyoutHost: the solo Report traffic in sight… flyout's menu row header
  FieldAdvisoryFlyout.cs        # Static Build and the FieldAdvisoryFlyoutHost row header: the Report field in sight… flyout (RFIS {clock} {miles}) in RPO and solo training, on the quick-command strip and in All Commands › Pilot reports; disabled as "· no destination field" (strip button faded and inert) when AdvisoryField.Of finds no destination
  ReportWhenFlyout.cs           # Static Build and ReportWhenFocus (MilesBox, FixBox): the one Report when… flyout (Report when…, At N-mile final…, At fix… alike): the Stop reporting rows and All reports while ArmedReports holds anything (MarkArmed puts the dot on the strip button), the title line, the turn rows in a pattern circuit, the Mile final chips 2/3/5/10 beside a checked miles box with an assigned runway, the Passing a fix box, then up to six reporting points (IFR: route fixes in order; else nearby visual points) with distance and magnetic bearing, each sending REPORT {NAME}
  FieldAdvisoryPanel.cs         # The field advisory flyout's panel (title, the Field advisory box with its ghost hint and RFIS command column, the field lead, the clock dial captioned field and the Miles stepper 1-999 that write the box and follow it, the spoken line, the always-not-checked line, the Enter button, the note, the field row with its live clock, miles and runways); pre-filled with the true values in RPO and in solo with True values, following aircraft updates until the user edits or sends; no radar cue
  AdvisoryField.cs              # Record: the destination field of an aircraft (Of: id, title-case name, reference point, every runway end) the sim's RFIS takes, RunwaysIn (the room's arrival ends, else every end) and TruthFrom (the true clock and miles through TrafficAdvisoryMatcher)
  SoloAdvisoryPanel.cs          # The solo Report traffic in sight… flyout's panel (title with a solo badge, the Advisory box with its ghost hint, the spoken line, the live check line, the folded Builder, the list of other airborne aircraft): Enter sends RTIS with the call over IMenuHost.SendForResultAsync and a second Enter in flight does nothing; the radar cue through RadarCueTracker
  SoloAdvisoryModel.cs          # AdvisoryCheck (state, segments, resolved callsign) and the live check's grading of the typed call against the matcher's candidates, plus MaxReportingPoints; the sim's TrafficAdvisoryMatcher decides, nothing here re-derives a match
  SoloAdvisoryText.cs           # AdvisoryForm (Clock, Nose, Pattern, Over), AdvisoryPart and Read: the typed words split into the form's parts, the ghost hints, the command preview and the command Enter sends; a complete call is decided by the sim's own parser
  SoloTrafficRowButton.cs       # One aircraft row of the solo advisory flyout: the pointed mark, callsign, type and leg, where the aircraft is (clock, miles, altitude difference, direction; with the pre-fill left for the student, only the altitude difference and find it on the scope) and your call on the resolved row; a click points, never sends
  AdvisoryBuilderView.cs        # The Builder's controls for one form: clock dial, miles stepper, direction pad, type, altitude with unknown; octant dial; pattern side and runway; the reporting points within the landmark radius
  AdvisoryBuilderParts.cs       # The Builder's round and stepper controls: AdvisoryRingPicker (a ring of buttons about a nose mark), AdvisoryClockDial, AdvisoryOctantDial and the whole-number stepper
  AdvisoryCandidates.cs         # Static From: the client's aircraft as the TrafficAdvisoryCandidates the sim builds (TrafficAdvisoryCandidate.From), the maneuvering flag and pattern position the sim's own
  GroundTrafficFlyout.cs        # Static BuildFollow / BuildGiveWay: the Follow… and Give way to… flyouts (strip flyout and All Commands) over IMenuHost.GetGroundTrafficRows, refilled through IStripFlyoutContent when a surface opens and, in the strip flyout, once a second while open (FlyoutLiveTick; the order held with the pointer inside, rows that left dimmed and disabled as gone rows, newcomers teal-edged; focus handed to the nearest live row). Give way: Nearest first title with the ▶/■ legend, eight rows then Show n more / Show fewer, GW. Follow: filter box and hint line, a scrolling Moving / Parked or holding list with a sticky title, box-first keyboard, FOLLOWG (the first typed word when nothing matches). A ScopeFeedbackTracker keeps the pointed row over the focused row for IMenuHost.HighlightAircraft and the ground views' ScopePreview (TrafficPreview builds it; a Give way row without a meet point says "no conflict predicted" while the aircraft has a route); with no traffic the entry is inert (QuickCommandStrip.MarkInert, MenuDisabledReasonRow)
  ScopePreview.cs               # Record RingCallsign/OwnRoute/TargetRoute/BarPoint/MeetPoint: what a pointed or focused menu row draws on the ground views (ring on an aircraft, own route solid, the other's dashed, a boxed bar, the amber meet point), every part optional; IMenuHost.SetScopePreview sends it, null clears
  ScopeFeedbackTracker.cs       # Internal: the pointed-over-focused row rule for a flyout's rows (Track, Unpoint, Clear, Refresh): drives IMenuHost.HighlightAircraft (only when built with highlightsAircraft: Hold short of… stops ring no aircraft) and SetScopePreview with the preview a callback builds for the row's key (a callsign, or a stop's HS text); Refresh resends the preview on the live refill
  RadarCue.cs                   # Records RadarCue(FromCallsign, TargetCallsign (null with no row pointed at: only the resolved aircraft's dashed ring), ResolvedCallsign, ShowBearingLabel) and ResolvedRadarCue(Target, ResolvedOther, From, BearingLabel): what a pointed or focused menu row draws on the radar views (a solid ring on the target, a dashed bearing line to it from the commanded aircraft with its `{clock} o'clock · {nm} nm` label, a dashed ring on the aircraft the call resolves to); ResolveAgainst places the ends against the aircraft a view draws; IMenuHost.SetRadarCue sends it, null clears
  RadarCueTracker.cs            # Internal: the pointed-over-focused row rule for the rows that drive a radar cue (Track, Clear): sets IMenuHost.SetRadarCue with the cue a callback builds for the row's key and highlights the cue's target
  FlyoutLiveTick.cs             # Internal: the once-a-second beat (Period) an open live flyout refills on, Start as it opens and Stop as it closes, on IMenuHost.Clock; a beat queued before the flyout closed or reopened does nothing
  PanelFlyoutContent.cs         # The base of the heading, altitude and speed flyouts: the internal IFlyoutPanel (View, Reset, Refresh, FocusOpeningLater), the public abstract FlyoutPanelHost (the menu row header holding the panel), and the internal PanelFlyoutContent (IStripFlyoutContent; Attach gives an entry its one panel row, builds the panel on first open, resets and refreshes it at each opening, ticks it on FlyoutLiveTick, and sends-and-closes through the host)
  FlyoutItemSync.cs             # Internal FlyoutItemSync.Sync(items, wanted): brings a live flyout's items to a wanted list in place, so a surviving row keeps pointer-over and focus (Push back to…, Follow…, Give way to…); FlyoutBoxFocus.HandFocusToTheBox: hands focus that lands on a row to the text box in its header (the takeoff flyout's boxes, Follow's filter)
  AircraftMenuBuilder.cs        # Static Build(aircraft, click, host, viewSection) (BuildPointMenu: the point menu, opening with SharedMenuGroups.AddPointHeader; AddPointStrip: its icon strip before its text items, the applicable ground point items or, on a radar point with an airborne aircraft, Fly heading, Direct to, the two holds and Warp here; AddFlyHeadingTurn: the dimmed `FH {hdg} · {left|right} turn, {n}°` row under the radar point menu's Fly heading): the aircraft context menu every view shows, in one order by the aircraft's kind (delayed spawn, surface live-traffic shadow, any other aircraft): header, the quick commands (strip, then text; the strip is grouped by category, and the release icons sit in Track and coordination via WithRelease; a delayed spawn gets just them in one flat row via AddReleaseStrip; with MenuContext.ShowQuickCommandIcons off there is no strip and AddQuickCommandRows lists the entries as text rows under category labels, the release rows among Track and coordination's, then custom entries after a separator, and the radar point menu's AddRadarPointRows does the same for its icons), Command… and Note…, All Commands (the full tree by task category in one fixed order, AddAllCommands; Vectors, Approach and Pattern hidden in a ground phase by HidesFlightCommands; the view section last after a separator, AddViewSection), Favorites, Delete
  QuickCommandEntry.cs          # Closed hierarchy: CatalogQuickCommandEntry (CatalogId + FlightRules?, null = catalog default) or CustomQuickCommandEntry (label, text, ground text?, FlightRules; MenuId "quick.custom", no glyph)
  QuickCommandDefaults.cs       # Static: each AircraftSituation's ordered default quick-command list (Unknown has none, so an unclassified aircraft shows no quick commands); catalog actions only, never a display item
  QuickCommandResolver.cs       # Static Resolve(aircraft, context, buildsAnItem) → QuickCommandResolution (strip items + text entries): the situation's list filtered by flight rules (honouring the VFR-commands-for-IFR setting), the catalog predicate or a quick-list Widening rule, and a quick-list Visibility rule; both tables apply only here, All Commands keeps every entry; a custom entry's row (BuildCustom) shows the first verb of the command its click would send for that aircraft as its alias
  QuickCommandGlyphs.cs         # Static: catalog ID → QuickCommandGlyph (24 px stroke path + QuickCommandGlyphFamily colour + RotationDegrees and MirroredTopToBottom, both 0/false here and set by PatternRunwayChoices.Glyph; HasForceMark: the amber "!" disc of the force pair) from the approved icon set, one for every default quick command (QuickCommandDefaultsTests.EveryDefaultQuickCommand_HasAGlyph); Split: every glyph-bearing entry forms the strip, the rest are text; PointById/ForPoint: the point menu's glyphs (the four ground point items and the five radar ones; Hold left and Hold right draw a racetrack with a turn-direction arrow), separate from ById
  QuickCommandCatalog.cs        # QuickCommandCatalogItem + the catalog actions the Quick Commands editor may add (id, family for grouping, label, default flight rules, glyph); display items and actions needing a selected or relative aircraft are not eligible
  PointMenuHostCache.cs         # IMenuHost wrapper for one point-menu open: answers Taxi here, runway hold-short targets, Custom taxi… and tug reach once per argument set (Taxi to runway passes through: its runway ends carry their own search) so the point strip and the text items share one set of host answers
  QuickCommandSituationNames.cs # Static: the name the Quick Commands editor shows for each classified AircraftSituation (Unknown has none); Classified lists them in enum order
  QuickCommandStripLayout.cs    # Static Pack(entries, categoryOf) → rows of segments (QuickCommandStripRow, QuickCommandStripSegment: one category's icons on one row, ShowsLabel false for the uncategorised group and a wrapped category's later rows): icons grouped by category in first-named order, uncategorised last, six to a row (RowLength), neighbouring partial groups sharing a row; the menu's strip and the Settings preview both pack through it
  QuickCommandStrip.cs          # Static Build: the icon strip, one MenuItem holding rows of six glyph buttons grouped by category (QuickCommandStripLayout), each group under a small upper-case label with a tick and rule, neighbouring groups sharing a row with a thin rule between (Draw; Layout reads back the rows a strip was laid out in); a button clicks its catalog entry's own built item, or opens that item's submenu as a flyout beside the menu, the button latched with the `sticky` class while it is open (corner notch; OpenSubmenu opens one as a click does); the pointed-at entry and its command (MenuCommandText) are named in the menu's header line, no label row or tooltip (MarkArmed puts the teal armed dot on an entry's button, ShowsArmedDot reads it) (QuickCommandLabelLine, a class in this file: the state line at rest, the label while a button is pointed at or focused, scrolled as a marquee when it overflows on its MarqueeClock; Ensure puts a blank one under the title when a menu has no state line; a private StripLabelTracker feeds it from the buttons); FromBuilt builds the grouped strip from built menu items, FlatFromBuilt the same ungrouped and unlabelled (the point menu's and a delayed spawn's release strip); GlyphIcon (with the force mark where the glyph has one), GlyphCell and Rows are the glyph, button-square and grouped row layout factories the strip and the Settings Quick Commands preview both draw with
  MenuAliases.cs                # Static: the command alias each catalog row shows (CanonicalVerbs: the verbs per id, any alias; NoVerb: ids with none; Display(id, scheme) and Display(verbs, scheme): each verb mapped to the scheme's primary verb, one alone, two as A / B, three or more as A …)
  MenuAlias.cs                  # Attached property MenuAlias.Text (the alias a row's MenuItem shows), MenuAlias.Apply (records it and sets the template on a plain-string-header row) and MenuAliasTemplate (label left, alias right in the dimmed monospace command font)
  MenuCommandText.cs            # Attached property on any Control: the command a sending menu item or a designed flyout's row button sends, recorded by MenuCatalog.BuildSend and the flyout builders so the strip's label can name it
  MenuCatalogEntry.cs           # Record: stable ID, label, default flight-rules filter, applicability predicate, builder → MenuItem? (aircraft nullable: a view builds the menu with no aircraft model when the clicked callsign has none); under All Commands the predicate alone decides whether it shows, in a quick list the resolver's widening and visibility rules also apply
  MenuClick.cs                  # Record: the click context — the commanded callsign, the previous selection (sender of the relative items), the clicked point (MenuPoint: position, ground node, runway end, the runways under a runway-surface click, the node Warp here uses there; null on an aircraft click), the list's selected rows
  MenuGlyphRow.cs               # Record MenuGlyphRow (Glyph, Label, optional Note, IsDimmed, Command) plus MenuGlyphRowTemplate.Instance: the view of a glyph row, the glyph drawn by QuickCommandStrip.GlyphIcon, a dimmed row faded to 0.5 and still clickable; the pattern entries' runway rows
  PatternRunwayChoices.cs       # Static: the runway ends a pattern entry's flyout offers (For: active arrival/both ends first, then the rest, landable before short in each group, runway order within), the glyph for an end (Glyph: lands-west glyph rotated by true course − 270°, Make straight-in mirrored for right traffic), the strip's glyph for an assigned runway (StripGlyph), and the room's active-runway reading (ReadRoomActiveRunways, ActiveArrivalEnds)
  MenuTwoLineRow.cs             # Record MenuTwoLineRow (Glyph, Label, Command, IsDimmed) and MenuTwoLineRowTemplate.Build: a two-line flyout row, the glyph beside an ellipsized label over the whole command in the monospace font, wrapped and never trimmed; ToString is the accessible name
  DirectToFlyout.cs             # The Direct to flyout's one builder (Build: strip and All Commands › Vectors › Route Direct to… / Append direct to…, each a DirectToVerb DCT / ADCT, registered through PanelFlyoutContent.Attach): DirectToFlyoutPanel (title with → next fix, Direct to / Append to route toggle while navigating, Fix box with BoxCommand and Not a known fix, the Remaining route rows and the All fixes · nearest first matches as you type, refreshed on FlyoutLiveTick), FixRow (● mark, fix, distance and magnetic bearing or passed, command), DirectToFlyoutHost
  FixList.cs                    # Static fix-list model the fix flyouts share: DirectToRoute (the route from the navigating-to fix on), DistanceAndBearing (31 NM · 184°, magnetic), Matches (FixesNear by prefix, nearest first, at most 50, however far), IsKnownFix (fix or FRD), Position
  FixValueFlyout.cs             # The Cross fix and Depart fix flyouts (BuildCross, BuildDepart: strip and All Commands › Vectors › Route, registered through PanelFlyoutContent.Attach; CrossFixAltitudeType, the CFIX / DEPART commands and captions, ParseAltitude / ParseHeading / SpeedRefusal, TypedFix): FixValueFlyoutPanel (title with → next fix, Fix box with Not a known fix, Route · next ● rows and All fixes · nearest first matches as you type, a row click or Enter with a value missing fills the Fix box and focuses the missing box, refreshed on FlyoutLiveTick), CrossFixFlyoutPanel (Cross at toggle, Altitude and optional Speed boxes), DepartFixFlyoutPanel (Heading after the fix box), FixRow (● mark, fix, distance and magnetic bearing or passed, the whole command ending ··· while a value is missing), CrossFixFlyoutHost, DepartFixFlyoutHost
  HoldFlyout.cs                 # The Hold at fix and 360s flyouts (BuildPattern: the strip's Hold at fix… / HOLDP, BuildTurns: All Commands › Approach holds block Left / Right 360s at fix… / HFIXL, HFIXR; HoldTurns, HoldLeg, StandardLeg (1 min at or below 14,000 ft MSL, 1½ above), DefaultCourse (magnetic, from the route fix before or from the aircraft), the caption and the four-token HOLDP command): HoldPatternFlyoutPanel (a FixValueFlyoutPanel with the Inbound course box, direction/radial, Legs chips and Turns toggle), HoldTurnsFlyoutPanel (Left / Right 360s toggle, fix box taking an FRD, the same fix list), HoldPatternFlyoutHost, HoldTurnsFlyoutHost
  JoinAirwayFlyout.cs           # The Join airway flyout (All Commands › Vectors › Route; Build): JoinAirwayFlyoutPanel (title, Airway box filtering rows by prefix with the unknown airway / military route: use CMTR refusals, caption Join V25, Filed ◆ rows then the airways through the route and fixes within 30 NM, nearest first, up to 12), JoinAirwayRow (the fix it proceeds to after intercepting on the present heading with distance and bearing, or no segment ahead); a click sends JAWY V25
  JoinRadialFlyout.cs           # The Join radial outbound / inbound flyout (All Commands › Vectors › Route; Build over RadialSide): JoinRadialFlyoutPanel (Outbound JRADO / Inbound JRADI toggle setting the title, Fix box taking a VOR, VORTAC or TACAN only, up to 12 Navaids · nearest first FixRows, Radial box (empty: the aircraft's radial), caption, a HeadingRose with the navaid in its centre and no turn arc); opens on the nearest route navaid within 130 NM else the nearest radial navaid; sends one token, JRADO RBL184, the radial magnetic by the station's variation
  JoinStarFlyout.cs             # The Join STAR flyout (strip and All Commands › Approach; Build over MenuCatalog.BuildJoinStar): Tabs (one JoinStarTab per STAR at the destination, the filed one first and marked ◆), JoinStarRow (Join, Join at {fix}, For runway {rwy}; the JARR command, no airport prefix), ServedRunways, JoinStarFlyoutPanel (title, FlyoutTabStrip, rows, Descend via STAR under the tabs) and JoinStarFlyoutHost
  PtacFlyout.cs                 # The PTAC flyout (All Commands › Approach › PTAC…; Build): PtacFlyoutPanel (title with hdg and altitude now, PTAC / Forced toggle, caption skip the intercept-angle check, Heading and Altitude boxes with PH / PA placeholders and Not a valid heading / altitude refusals, Approach group of PtacRows: The expected approach (● {id} expected, sends no id) then the destination's approaches from ApproachChoices, each showing its whole PTAC / PTACF command or ··· while a box is invalid, amber while Forced; No destination filed / No approaches at {dest} notes; Enter in a box sends the command without an approach id), PtacRow, PtacFlyoutHost
  CoordinationFlyout.cs         # The Initiate handoff… (HO) and Point out… (PO) flyouts (Track; Build over CoordinationVerb, registered through PanelFlyoutContent.Attach): Arrange (the student's own position on top unless it owns the track, then the staffed positions from IMenuHost.GetPositions grouped by facility: the student's, Towers, the Center, the rest; Point out lists only positions with a TCP), Matches (prefix of code, name, a word of the name, callsign or frequency), CoordinationFlyoutPanel (title with the aircraft and its owner, the box with HO ··· / PO ···, the Not a staffed position: sent as typed note; Enter on no match sends the typed code), CoordinationRow (code · name · frequency — command; the owner's row greyed, owns the track, never sent), CoordinationFlyoutHost; a row sends the server's HandoffCode for HO and the TCP for PO
  WarpFlyout.cs                 # The Warp flyout's one builder (Build: Warp… in All Commands with an empty FRD box, Warp here at a map point with WarpFrdSeed.ClickedPoint; airborne controllable aircraft only): WarpTitleLine (Warp, then callsign · now heading° · altitude ft · speed kt), four InlineBoxRow.CreateSendingAll rows (Fix or FRD with fix suggestions, Heading, Altitude (hundreds, feet or FL), Speed; a blank field before a filled one sends its present value, a trailing blank is left out, a sub-100 ft present altitude refuses a blank Altitude before a filled Speed) and WarpSendLine (the whole WARP command, or can't send); Enter in a box and the send line send
  FixListView.cs               # The fix list the Direct to, Cross fix, Depart fix, Hold and Join radial flyouts share: FixRow (one row: fix, distance/bearing, its command, the next-fix dot, passed latched), the route and match rows with their headers, the outline, the live refresh and the Up/Down/Enter walk; each flyout supplies a FixListBehaviour (command for a fix, extra match rows, choose, in-a-box, Enter)
  FlyoutPanelParts.cs           # Internal static: the plain panel-row style (PlainPanelRowStyle) and the separator line (SeparatorLine) the Taxi to runway, Air taxi / Land and approach flyouts share; Refusal (the red reason line) and RefusableBox (a box that refuses its text inline: its frame kept, a 2 px red bar over its bottom edge, a reason line to set under it; InlineBoxRow uses it); the VerbToggle / ToggleStrip, BoxRow (with SetBoxRowGlyph), Chip and CommandText the Fly heading and Direct to flyouts build on
  FlyoutTabStrip.cs             # FlyoutTabState (Ready, Searching, Empty), record FlyoutTab (Label, State) and FlyoutTabStrip: the tab strip above a designed flyout's rows, wrapping in order; SetTabs, SetState, Select, Move(-1/+1, no wrap, false at an end), SelectionChanged; Searching shows a spinner (finite IterationCount, since Animation.RunAsync faults an infinite one), Empty a dimmed italic `none`; Tabs and AccessibleName (`28R`, `28R, searching`, `12, none`); each tab is a TabItem for UI Automation whose invoke selects it, never keyboard focusable; IsSpinning(index) reports the animation gate for tests, not the logical state
  MenuCommandRow.cs             # Records MenuCommandRow (optional Glyph, Name, EmphasizeName, IsHighlighted (bold row), Detail with DetailPlacement Stacked/Route (Route: the taxiways in mono under the name, row capped at 480 px, command at 180 px, all trimmed with the whole row in the tooltip), Distance, Command; ToString is the row's one-line text) plus MenuCommandRowTemplate.Instance: the one view of a clickable command row, used by the Hold short of… rows through MenuCatalog.BuildTemplatedSend; also record MenuDisabledReasonRow (Label, Reason; ToString `Follow… · no other aircraft on the ground`) with MenuDisabledReasonRowTemplate and Apply(item, label, reason): a disabled entry that says why beside its label
                                # RowMaxWidth (480 px) is the width cap of every menu: App.axaml.cs Initialize overrides Fluent's FlyoutThemeMaxWidth with it; a route row past the cap is ellipsized and shown whole in its tooltip
  RunwayHoldShortTarget.cs      # Record Node/Label: one hold-short target a runway-surface click offers per runway end ("At B (nearest)", "Full length (at W)")
  MenuContext.cs                # Record: the click (MenuClick) and the session (MenuSession), with read-through Callsign / PreviousSelection / Initials / SoloTrainingMode / VfrCommandsForIfr / ShowQuickCommandIcons — the predicates' non-aircraft inputs
  MenuFlightRules.cs            # Enum Both/IfrOnly/VfrOnly: a quick-command entry's default flight-rules filter
  MenuIds.cs                    # Stable menu IDs, <group>.<item>, one per action, never renamed or reused (exported preferences carry them; a retired id is deleted and a saved list drops it with a logged warning)
  MenuCategories.cs             # Enum MenuCategory (LiveTraffic, Runway, Taxi, Vectors, Approach, Pattern, PilotReports, TrackAndCoordination, Sim: the seven task categories are the All Commands groups, in order; LiveTraffic and Sim label only the quick commands) and static MenuCategories: Label (a category's label), Of (a catalog id's category, null for none) and Uncategorized (the ids in no category: root items, spawn, point-menu and For-section items); MenuCategoriesTests holds every MenuCatalog id to exactly one of the two
  MenuRichList.cs               # Record Title/Subtitle/Rows/SelectedIndex: a titled picker of rich rows, opened on SelectedIndex
  MenuRichRow.cs                # Record Glyph/Label/Hint/Kind/Command/Value: one rich-picker row; a null Value is never a type-ahead target
  MenuRichRowKind.cs            # Enum Climb/Descend/Now/Assigned/NowAssigned/BelowMva/MvaLine: a rich row's mark and colour
  MenuAirborneTrafficRow.cs     # Record Traffic (a MenuTrafficRow)/Direction/Leg: one other airborne aircraft as the Report traffic in sight… and Follow traffic… lists show it
  MenuGroundTrafficRow.cs       # Record Callsign/AircraftType/DistanceFeet/State + IsMoving/IsSurfaceShadow/MeetPoint (the commanded aircraft's meet point with it, null for none): one other ground aircraft as the Follow… and Give way to… lists show it, seen from the commanded aircraft
  NearbyReportingPoint.cs       # Record Name/DistanceNm/BearingTrueDeg: one reporting point the Report when flyout offers near an aircraft (IMenuHost.GetReportingPointsNearby), with the ReportWhenNearbyRadiusNm (10 nm) and ReportWhenMaxNearbyPoints (6) limits
  MenuTrafficRow.cs             # Record Callsign/AircraftType/DistanceNm/ClockPosition/AltitudeDeltaFeet: one other aircraft as the Report traffic in sight… and Follow traffic… lists show it (the core of a MenuAirborneTrafficRow), seen from the commanded aircraft
  RelativeGeometry.cs           # Static: clock position from true heading, altitude delta rounded to 100 ft, whole nm, and the wording of the For section's line and a traffic row's columns; TrafficRow, Describe, GroundTrafficRow and DescribeGround build them from two IMenuAircraft (ground: feet rounded to 50/100, a state column, ahead within 90 degrees of heading)
  MenuMeasureState.cs           # Enum None/NoAnchor/HasAnchor: a view's measure tool as `CanvasMenuItems.Measure` reads it (None = no tool, no item)
  MenuPickerDescriptor.cs       # Tag on a picker menu item: the free-text input popup the item opens, readable without opening it (the menu goldens print it)
  MenuSession.cs                # Record: the menu session — initials, solo training mode, VFR-for-IFR mode, the solo advisory settings (SoloAdvisoryPrefill, SoloAdvisoryLiveCheck), the command scheme, ShowQuickCommandIcons (icon strips or text rows)
  RunwayDesignatorComparer.cs   # Orders runway designators by number then L/C/R (the runway flyout and the menu runway pickers)
  RunwayDesignators.cs          # Static: ForAirport — an airport's runway ends in display form, sorted, for the visual-approach and pattern pickers
  SharedMenuGroups.cs           # Static: the menu's groups, built from catalog entries in one order and text on every view: the header (`AddHeader`: the title `{callsign} · {type}`, the state line (phase, altitude, speed and runway airborne; phase, taxiway or parking spot and airport on the ground; phase, runway and ground speed on a landing roll), route summary and hold status rows: information rows only; the state line is a QuickCommandLabelLine), `AddCommandAndNote` (Command…, Note…), favorites, the live-traffic assume items, the All Commands task groups (`Tower` as Runway, `Vectors` over the `Heading`, `Altitude`, `Speed` and `Route` blocks, `Approach` over its pickers, STAR/PTAC and `Hold` blocks, `Pattern`, `PilotReports`, `TrackAndCoordination` over the `Track` (the groups the track's own state calls for, a separator between the groups shown), `DataBlock`, `Squawk` and `Coordination` blocks; a group with nothing in it is null), the For section (`AddForSection`, opening the menu under the header), Edit flight plan, the sim-control items that close All Commands (`AddSimControl`: Warp…, Release to live feed), the foot (`AddFoot`: Delete), a delayed aircraft's menu (`AddDelayedSpawn`), the multi-select assume (`AddAssumeSelected`) and the point menu's header (`AddPointHeader`: the title `{callsign} · {type} → {place}` and, on a radar point (`IsRadarPoint`), the dimmed FRD, distance and bearing row; `PointHeaderShowsFrd` tells the radar to leave out its own FRD row; `DetailRow` builds the dimmed row the header, the Fly heading turn row and the radar's MVA row use); AircraftMenuBuilder places them, including a surface live-traffic shadow's read-only menu (its private `AddSurfaceShadow`: Favorites, All Commands holding only Track and coordination without Squawk or Edit flight plan and then the view section after a separator, then the foot)
  TaxiRouteDisplayMode.cs       # Enum Follow/AlwaysShow/AlwaysHide: an aircraft's per-aircraft taxi-route override in the ground view (the Taxi route radio submenu)
  SpawnDelay.cs                 # The Change spawn delay presets and the custom box's parser (90, 2m15s, 1h → seconds)
  HoldShortMenuHelper.cs        # The held runway from the "Holding Short {rwy}" phase (IsHoldingShort: any holding-short phase, which also gates Hold short of…), for the Cross, Line up and wait and Cleared for takeoff items on every view
  RelativeTraffic.cs            # Relative-selection gates over IMenuAircraft: HasRelativeContext, the ground pair (ShouldOfferGroundActions, OffersGroundRelative, both on the ground and the selected aircraft controllable), the airborne pair (both airborne, controllable) and ShouldOfferFollow (the selected aircraft reported the clicked one in sight); SharedMenuGroups.AddForSection builds the For section on every view

Models/
  TerminalColorScheme.cs        # Operator-tunable per-Kind terminal foreground colors (Command/Response/System/Say/PilotSpeech/Warning/Error/Chat/Tdls/Strip); defaults match the legacy hard-coded scheme
  CommandHistoryEntry.cs        # Up-arrow recall entry: (Callsign, Command). Callsign-less command text + the aircraft it was sent to (empty = global/untargeted); recall filters by selected aircraft
  DatablockDeconflictMode.cs    # Per-view datablock-deconfliction setting (Off / Snap / Free-form); persisted per view in UserPreferences
  GroundColorScheme.cs          # Theme/color scheme for strips
  RendererMode.cs               # User-selectable GPU backend (Auto/Metal/OpenGl/Software); macOS-only — the default OpenGL path is emulated over Metal and burns CPU on Apple Silicon, so Metal renders directly; ignored on Windows/Linux
  TerminalEntry.cs              # Terminal/radio log entry (Kind: Command/Response/System/Say/Warning/Error/Chat/PilotSpeech/Tdls/Strip); Sequence (process-wide counter, a log position that survives trimming; LastSequence reads the newest) and IsHistory (a line rebuilt from a loaded recording)
  LiveTrafficListFilter.cs      # Aircraft List treatment of live-traffic shadows (All / HideLive / OnlyLive); persisted in UserPreferences

ViewModels/
  ConnectViewModel.cs           # Room/identity connection flow

Views/
  ConnectWindow.axaml.cs        # Server/room/identity entry dialog
  WindowGeometryHelper.cs       # Save/restore window position+size+min/max/topmost state; profile-apply path (ApplyGeometry) un-minimizes + activates (#365); post-open drift verify re-applies the saved spot, and the save path never persists a position from a window with no platform surface (#408); composes WindowSystemMenuHelper + WindowNativeMenuHelper for cross-platform always-on-top discoverability; attaches every window to WindowGroupRaiser; in automation mode applies ShowActivated=false, never activates, keeps the requested pin without setting Topmost, and keeps windows Normal (a saved Maximized/Minimized state is remembered for saving, not applied, since any visible WindowState change activates on Win32)
  WindowGroupRaiser.cs          # CRC-style group raise (#392): when focus returns from another app, raises all tracked windows via Topmost pulses (no focus steal) in Z-order, clicked window last; suspended during profile apply; gated by the RaiseWindowsTogether preference (default on); never raises in automation mode
  WindowActivationExtensions.cs # Window.RestoreAndActivate(): un-minimize (WindowState.Normal) before Activate — every reuse-and-activate window (FPE, Favorites Panel, Speech Debug, Session Report, Weather/Arrival editors) goes through this (#360); skips Activate in automation mode
  DialogPresenter.cs            # ShowModalAsync[<T>](dialog, owner): every client dialog opens through it. Outside automation mode it is ShowDialog; in automation mode the dialog shows never-activated and non-modal, the owner is disabled by hand (one count per owner) and its user close cancelled (ShouldCancelOwnerClose), and the result comes back on Closed. Result dialogs close through DialogPresenter.Close(this, value); AutomationModeSourceTests rejects a raw ShowDialog or a bare Close(result)
  AutomationGate.cs             # Core-side automation-mode flag (SuppressActivation, set through Yaat.Client's AutomationMode) and ApplyShowActivated(window) (ShowActivated=false plus WS_EX_NOACTIVATE through the Win32 window-styles callback, NoActivateStyles; on Windows also SetWindowPos to the bottom of the Z order before the first show, and an Opened handler that places an owned window directly above its owner; with CloakWindows set, a DWM cloak before the first show through the Cloaker seam, exit 3 on failure) for windows that do not build a WindowGeometryHelper and for every DialogPresenter dialog; AutomationModeSourceTests keeps every Activate()/Topmost=true behind it
  WindowSystemMenuHelper.cs     # Windows-only: injects "Always on Top" into the title-bar system menu via WM_SYSCOMMAND + SetWindowSubclass
  WindowNativeMenuHelper.cs     # macOS-only: adds "Window → Always on Top" to the menu bar via Avalonia NativeMenu
  KeybindHelper.cs              # Keyboard shortcut resolution
  IAlwaysOnTopToggle.cs         # Window contract for the central always-on-top hotkey (WindowHotkeys); each window delegates to its WindowGeometryHelper.ToggleTopmost
  VStrips/VStripsViewWindow.axaml.cs # Pop-out window shell for the strips view — sizes/positions/titles itself; content (a VStripsSplitHost) is supplied by the creating host. Stays in Core because it depends on UserPreferences + WindowGeometryHelper + KeybindHelper; only the desktop hosts open this Window.
  VTdls/VTdlsViewWindow.axaml.cs # Pop-out window wrapper for VTdlsView. Same shape as VStripsViewWindow: per-facility geometry key (`VTdlsView:{facilityId}`), first-time topmost inherited from the global `VTdlsView` default, AlwaysOnTop hotkey from UserPreferences.
```

## Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis — raw mouse input (`src/Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis/`)

Hover and drag as raw mouse input through a window's own platform input callback, so Avalonia's input manager treats it as a real mouse (pointer-over, capture, drag threshold) while the real cursor and the foreground never change. References only the `Avalonia` package (no Yaat.Sim, no Yaat.Client.Core) and opts into Avalonia's private raw-input API in its own project alone; `Yaat.Client` is its only referrer. Namespace `Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis`.

```
Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis.csproj # net10.0; Avalonia 12.1.0; AvaloniaAccessUnstablePrivateApis + AVA3001 suppressed here only
SyntheticMouse.cs             # MouseDrag record (From, To, Button, Steps, HoldMs) and SyntheticMouse: HoverAsync (one move onto the point, a rest, the pointer stays there) and a drag (press, equal moves StepInterval = 16 ms apart, a hold, release) raised through ITopLevelImpl.Input on a per-TopLevel MouseDevice kept for the window's life; GestureInProgress(topLevel) names the hover or drag under way; points are DIPs from the client area's top-left; UI thread
```

## Yaat.Client — Avalonia desktop app (`src/Yaat.Client/`)

```
Automation/
  AutomationHost.cs             # The pipe host: a NamedPipeTransport (current user only) + DiscoveryFile + AutomationDispatcher over one NodeRegistry and the AutomationSources it is given (constructor: pipe name, discovery directory, sources); started from App.OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted on Windows in automation mode (AutomationHostFactory.StartIfEnabled), disposed on exit and Ctrl+C. Derived from Zafiro.Avalonia.Mcp (MIT, NOTICE)
  AutomationSources.cs          # AutomationSources(Roots, State, Tools, Time): what the host drives, the windows, the simulation state, the app tools and the clock the wait handlers time on; the app passes TimeProvider.System (AutomationHostFactory), a test a FakeTimeProvider
  AutomationHostFactory.cs      # StartIfEnabled guard, PipeName(pid) = yaat-automation-<pid>, DiscoveryDirectory = %TEMP%/yaat-automation (a deliberate exception to YaatPaths, so a driver finds every client)
  AutomationDispatcher.cs       # Reads one JSON request per line, routes by method to an IRequestHandler, answers a result or a coded AutomationError (malformed / missing id / unknown method → INVALID_PARAM, handler exception → INTERNAL, logged); every answer carries in clientErrors the errors the client logged from the call's start to its answer (AppLog.RecentErrors, at most AutomationResponse.MaxClientErrors)
  FilePickQueue.cs              # The process-wide FIFO of file-dialog answers (FilePickAnswer: a path or a cancel) that queue_file_pick fills and the injected picker drains; Enqueue returns the length after the enqueue, all under one lock
  InjectedFilePickerService.cs  # Automation mode's IFilePickerService: no TopLevel or StorageProvider, each call takes one FilePickQueue answer (path, or null / [] for a cancel); an empty queue throws InvalidOperationException at once
  IAutomationState.cs           # The simulation state the pipe reads (aircraft, scenario seconds, pause, sim rate, terminal cursor and entries since it) and the client actions it may take (PauseAsync, UnpauseAsync, SetRateAsync, as AutomationActionOutcome); UI thread only
  MainViewModelAutomationState.cs # IAutomationState over MainViewModel: terminal entries by TerminalEntry.Sequence, actions send raw PAUSE / UNPAUSE / SIMRATE through the server connection
  Handlers/                     # IRequestHandler, HandlerResult, PingHandler (pid + protocol version), ListWindowsHandler (the provider's windows, their owned windows, their overlay popups, and each in-window overlay Border carrying the `window-overlay` class while it shows (a popup row titled by its AutomationProperties.Name), as WindowInfo with node ids), TreeHandler (get_tree: Visual/Logical, depth, a nodeId or selector root, each window's overlay popups as their own roots, listed once); input: ClickHandler (Avalonia's own click through the automation peers' IInvokeProvider/IToggleProvider, menu items and selection, the synthetic pointer only when no meaningful action exists), ClickPointHandler (window-relative DIPs, InputHitTest, one pointer press/release), SendKeysHandler + SendKeysParser (today's MCP SendKeys syntax: text events at the caret, routed KeyDown/KeyUp for braced keys and Ctrl/Alt prefixes, Shift on a letter or digit types the shifted character, push-to-talk refused), SetTextHandler, FocusHandler, SyntheticPointer, InputParams, TargetResolver; HoverHandler (hover: rests a raw mouse on a selector's element centre or a window point for durationMs, the pointer stays) and DragHandler (drag: press, equal moves, hold and release with a button held; both points inside the window's client area), both over Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis, answering HoverResult / DragResult, UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION while a hover or drag is still under way in that window, OUT_OF_BOUNDS outside the client area; PointerTargets (the window point or element centre both resolve through, the Busy and Inside checks, the receiver under a point, the PointerSite the results carry; ClickPointHandler resolves through it too); WaitUntilHandler (wait_until: polls IAutomationState every 100 ms for on_ground / landed / phase_is / phase_is_not / queue_empty / log_matches / client_log_matches (ClientLogTail reads yaat-client.log off the UI thread) / sim_seconds / paused conditions, any or all, answers met or not met as a WaitUntilResult, captures the optional screenshot and runs the then actions pause / unpause / set_rate at the matching poll) + WaitUntilParams (request parsing and validation: WaitUntilCondition, WaitUntilAction, timeout clamped to 100-600000 ms); ListAppToolsHandler (list_app_tools: every app tool with its parameters and current availability) and CallAppToolHandler (call_app_tool: binds a JSON object by parameter name, INVALID_PARAM for an unknown tool or a missing / unknown / wrong-kind argument, checked before availability)
  AutomationToolAttribute.cs    # [AutomationTool(name, description, availabilityMethod)]: marks an AutomationTools method as an app tool the pipe lists and calls; the availability method is a public parameterless string? (null = available)
  Tools/                        # AutomationTools (partial per area: Connection, Sim, Radar, Session, Recording, Measure, DataBlock, Window, Pointer, Coaching, Command, Pause) over MainViewModel + IAutomationState: the coaching student's scan tools get_traffic, get_aircraft, read_terminal, get_airport_context and center_ground (AutomationTools.Coaching.cs: compact JSON, null fields omitted), send_command (AutomationTools.Command.cs: the command box's Enter path through MainViewModel.SubmitCommandTextAsync, answering LastSubmission, the status line and new warnings), arm_pause_triggers / disarm_pause_triggers / get_pause_events (AutomationTools.Pause.cs over Automation/PauseTriggerMonitor.cs: one monitor per MainViewModel, a 200 ms UI-thread check that pauses the sim on a pilot's own call, a warning or conflict, a new aircraft, a ground stop or a heartbeat, and logs each event); the app tools hover and drag (AutomationTools.Pointer.cs: the same raw-mouse gestures as the pipe methods, by window selector and DIPs), set_cloaked (available whenever the main window is up; the window list through the OpenWindows seam), connect, create_room, set_sim_rate, play, pause, center_radar, center_radar_at, center_radar_on_fix, set_video_map, set_ptl, get_framing, set_solo, load_recording, seek, prepare_take (load, wait for maps, seek, pause, then FrameTake applies maps/centre/range/PTL/RBLs; RunTakeStepsAsync is the test seam), place_rbl, remove_rbl, clear_rbls (primary radar only), set_leader_direction, set_datablock_offset, reset_datablock_offset; the catalog is read by reflection once; a tool rejects its own arguments with AppToolArgumentException (INVALID_PARAM)
  Protocol/                     # The wire types (AutomationRequest/Response/Error/ErrorCodes, ClientLogEntry (one client error in a response's clientErrors: level, category, message, exception), DiscoveryInfo, NodeInfo, WindowInfo, PingResult, WaitUntilResult (met flag, held indices, last value per condition, optional screenshot and then-action outcomes), AppToolInfo, CallAppToolParams, CallAppToolResult, GetSimTimeResult (sim seconds, paused flag, sim rate), ProtocolMethods, ProtocolSerializer, ProtocolVersion (1.5.0), InputResults (PointerSite, HoverResult, DragResult), ErrorDetails; WindowInfo carries each window's native handle, 0 when there is none) — no Avalonia dependency, so the client-driver MCP (yaat-server) can link the files as source
  Transport/                    # NamedPipeTransport (PipeOptions.CurrentUserOnly, one task per client, 100 ms back-off on a failed accept), DiscoveryFile (atomic write of <pid>.json, delete on dispose, sweep of dead / reused-pid / unparsable files and orphaned .tmp)
  Selectors/                    # SelectorParser (CSS-like: Type, *, #Name, #42 node id, [P=v] / *= ^= $=, [dc.Prop=v] over a locally set DataContext, descendant/child combinators, :nth on the last compound, :visible/:enabled/:has-text/:role…, comma alternatives; errors with positions), SelectorEngine (exact type or base type, UI thread only), SelectorRequestHelper (TryResolveSingle → MISSING/INVALID_SELECTOR, NO_MATCH, AMBIGUOUS_SELECTOR, STALE_NODE)
  Tree/NodeRegistry.cs          # Per-host stable node ids over weak references to visuals (UI thread only; dead entries pruned)
  Tree/NodeInfoBuilder.cs       # Builds NodeInfo (type, name, bounds, text, role, state, parent/owner ids, children to a depth)
  Tree/ElementDescription.cs    # The one role table and text reader get_tree reports and selectors match (text falls back to AutomationProperties.Name)
  AutomationMode.cs             # Automation mode switch: on when YAAT_AUTOMATION=1 (ReadFromEnvironment, set once in Program.Main), plus the cloak switch YAAT_CLOAK=1 (ReadCloakFromEnvironment; Program.ApplyCloakSwitch exits 2 when it is set without automation); IsEnabled drives AutomationGate.SuppressActivation, and Program turns off the global key hook and Discord Rich Presence and enables Win32 OverlayPopups

Models/
  AircraftModel.cs              # ObservableObject wrapping AircraftDto; computed displays; FromDto/UpdateFromDto; CruiseMach/IsSpeedClassified show a filed Mach (`M078`) or classified (`SC`) speed read-only via FiledSpeedDisplay, EditorSpeedText, EditorSpeedPlaceholder
  AircraftSpeechBubble.cs       # Per-aircraft speech bubble model for opt-in SAY/pilot (green) and WARN (amber) overlays on Radar/Ground views (text, severity, user-scaled duration or persist-until-clicked, dismiss state).
  FlightPlanAmendment.cs        # Immutable flight-plan amendment record (type/suffix/route/altitude/beacon/scratchpad) built by the flight plan editor and dispatched to the server

Services/

  ServerConnection.cs           # SignalR client to /hubs/training (JSON); inline DTOs

  ScrollStepAccumulator.cs      # Converts a burst of mouse-wheel events into whole discrete steps scaled by UserPreferences.ScrollSensitivity — slows DCB range/PTL/history/brightness spinners on a fast Mac trackpad (#275)

  PiperVoiceInstaller.cs        # Settings-driven Piper voice-pack downloader/extractor into YaatPaths app data.
  PilotVoiceService.cs          # Off-by-default solo-training pilot voice queue. Consumes PilotTransmissionBroadcastDto FIFO; synthesizes with sherpa-onnx/Piper, applies NAudio.Dsp radio FX, plays through PortAudio.

PendingUploadIds/MarkUploaded/ClearPendingUploads manage the markers; WriteBundle zips chosen samples to a stream.

  SavedWeatherGrid.cs           # Pure JSON helpers for the grid a saved weather file carries: Embed (windGridGrib2 base64 beside windGridHash), Extract (bytes out, JSON never carries them; no usable bytes strips the hash), NamedHash, NamesGrid, WithHash
  WeatherSaveGrid.cs            # Save Weather's file contents: the JSON unchanged when it names no grid, the held bytes embedded when they match, else the room's grid fetched by hash (size cap, hash and decode checked) and embedded; a failed fetch strips the hash and returns the reason for the "Saved without its GFS grid" line
  ArtccAirportResolver.cs       # Fetches vNAS ARTCC config → underlying airport IDs (cached); recurses the facility tree for every STARS config
  LiveSessionAirportDefaults.cs # Pure: which airports the live-session picker offers for a position (tower-cab airports under its facility, else the ARTCC) and the default
  FdRegionMapping.cs            # Static ARTCC → FD region code mapping
  UserPreferences.cs            # JSON to the static FilePath, default YaatPaths.AppDataRoot/preferences.json (%LOCALAPPDATA%/yaat/); UseFile(path) switches it for instances created afterwards, and an instance reads and writes only the file it was created on (incl. PilotVoiceEnabled/Volume/RadioFxEnabled (default off), DiscordRichPresenceEnabled (default on), macros, loaded favorite-set ids and the favorite-set bar order FavoriteSetOrder (FavoriteSetsChanged event; not a bundled preference) + legacy favorites fields kept readable for FavoriteLegacyMigration, favorite video maps per ARTCC/airport/scenario, favorite METAR stations per scenario, and the macOS renderer-backend override read by Program.BuildAvaloniaApp); PreferencesVersion gates one-time load migrations (MigratePreferences) — v0→v1 resets AutoArrivalSpacingOnOccupiedRunwayTwr to its new off default for a file saved before the field existed
  AtomicFile.cs                 # WriteAllText: .tmp write then replacing move, retried ~0.7 s while another process (an antivirus scan) briefly holds the target; used by UserPreferences and FavoriteStore
  FavoriteStore.cs              # Identity model for favorite commands: FavoriteCommand entities (8-hex ids) + FavoriteSet containers (Global/Airport/Scenario/Named, id-list membership), file-per-entity persistence under %LOCALAPPDATA%/yaat/favorites/ ([Label].{id}.json, rename-follows-label), ComposeDisplay (visible containers in the stored bar order, FavoriteSetOrdering.Normalize), DuplicateIntoSet (independent copies of favorites appended to a set) and DuplicateSet (a set and its favorites copied into a new unloaded named set), Changed event
  FavoriteSetOrdering.cs        # The bar order of the favorite sets: one list of slot tokens (global/airport/scenario) and named-set ids, loaded or not; Normalize (the stored order is never trusted: missing slots first, missing sets last), IsSlot, and the FavoriteSetRow record behind a Sets-flyout row
  FavoriteLegacyMigration.cs    # One-time conversion of pre-identity favorites (scope-field base pool, embedded named sets, loaded names) into FavoriteStore; maps window-profile loaded-set names to ids and drops the legacy preferences fields
  FavoriteExport.cs             # Zip sharing: [Name].yaat-favset.zip (set.json + favorites/[Label].{id}.json) and .yaat-favlibrary.zip (all sets + all favorites + library.json with loaded ids and setOrder; Replace restores the order, Merge keeps the current one with new sets last); import takes a FavoriteImportMode — Merge by id (scope sets merge into local containers, named-set name collisions auto-suffix) or Replace (FavoriteStore.Clear after the file parses, then the file's sets become the loaded set); Merge reports each set's incoming → final id (FavoriteImportResult.SetIdMap)
  SettingsBundle.cs             # Settings bundle model: SettingsItemType (preferences, macros, verbs, favorites, grid layout, layouts), SettingsBundleEntry, SettingsBundleFormats (format names, legacy single-item extensions, which types Merge), SettingsBundle (a read bundle + skipped entries)
  SettingsBundleFile.cs         # *.yaat-settings.zip reader/writer: manifest.json (bundleVersion, writtenBy, entries); a legacy single-item file reads as a one-entry bundle; a single ticked legacy type exports as its legacy file; newer bundles import known entries and report the rest
  BoundedZipReader.cs           # Zip reads capped while copying (32 MB an entry, 128 MB a file) for bundles and the favorites zip inside them
  SettingsBundleItems.cs        # Per-item adapters: build an entry from today's state (byte-identical to the legacy serializers) and read one back, rejecting null or nameless macros/layouts and a layout whose favorite set order lists an empty entry
  SettingsImportPlanner.cs      # Import plans: Merge clashes (macros by base name, favorite sets by name or id, layouts by name) with Skip/Overwrite/Rename and suggestions; ValidateRenames; ApplyAll (favorites first, then layouts' LoadedFavoriteSetIds and FavoriteSetOrder remapped)
  SettingsImportTarget.cs       # ISettingsImportTarget (where an import writes: UserPreferencesImportTarget, or the Settings view model's staged rows)
  SettingsExportSource.cs       # ISettingsExportSource (what an export reads, one entry per item type): UserPreferencesExportSource over preferences, favorites and the command scheme
  UserPreferences.Bundle.cs     # The preferences a bundle carries: an allowlist of Settings-section keys, each with a BundledPreferenceRule (range, colour, enum, keybind, model-source checks) that rejects a bad value before anything is written; UnbundledKeys names every excluded key and why
  BundledPreferenceRule.cs      # One allowlisted preference's validation rule
  CommandSchemeFile.cs          # Shareable command-verb file (*.yaat-verbs.json, full scheme keyed by CanonicalCommandType name) — Serialize/Deserialize behind Settings › Command verbs Import/Export; unknown command names are reported, not thrown
  QuickCommandListsFile.cs      # Shareable quick-command lists (*.yaat-quickcommands.json: version, situations by name, tagged catalog/custom entries) — overrides only; Replace and per-situation Merge (Skip/Overwrite); unreadable entries dropped and reported
  MenuGroup.cs                  # Enum of context menu groups (Heading, Altitude, Speed, Tower, etc.)
  ContextMenuProfile.cs         # Record: Primary/Secondary/Hidden menu groups for a phase
  ContextMenuProfileService.cs  # Static: maps phase name + isOnGround → ContextMenuProfile (which radar submenu GROUPS show)
  AircraftCommandApplicability.cs # Static: single source of truth for whether a tower/ground/landing/pattern command fits an aircraft's state (CanClearForTakeoff/CanClearToLand/CanIssueVfrOption/CanEnterPattern/CanEnterFinal/CanIssuePatternManeuvers/CanExitRunway/CanDrawTaxiRoute/...), all false for a live-traffic shadow (IsControllable) which only gets CanAssume; consumed by all three right-click surfaces + phase classifiers used by ContextMenuProfileService. The VfrCommandsForIfr-aware predicates are enforcement, not clutter suppression — the sim does not gate on flight rules
  VfrCommandGate.cs             # Static: checks a canonical command against the controller's VfrCommandsForIfr setting before MainViewModel.SendCommandAsync puts it on the wire; re-parses via CommandParser so no verb list is duplicated (issue #317)
  BuildInfo.cs                  # Static: version (from AssemblyInformationalVersion) + release-vs-dev detection (VelopackLocator.Current); used by title bar, About window, and startup log line
  DocLinks.cs                   # Static: GitHub URLs for user-facing docs (README/USER_GUIDE/COMMANDS/CHANGELOG/issues), pinned to release tag for installed builds, main for dev
  UrlLauncher.cs                # Static: opens HTTPS URLs in OS default browser (Process.Start with UseShellExecute)
  CrcAliasExecutor.cs           # Plans an expanded CRC alias into a CrcAliasExecution (Echo/ScopeMarkers/OpenUrl/Unsupported/Failed) — substitutes variables first, then reads the verb, matching CRC's ordering. .echo expands \n/\s/\t into terminal lines; .openurl takes only the first token and returns AbsoluteUri (ToString would un-escape and undo $urlescape); .am/.msg/.autotrack/.wallop and verb-less prose report unsupported
  CallsignPrefixResolver.cs     # Pure resolver: partial callsign prefix → unique aircraft or list of matching candidates. Used by MainViewModel.SendCommandAsync to disambiguate `N12` when multiple aircraft match. A leading known command verb (via CommandScheme.IsKnownVerb) is never treated as a partial callsign — only an exact match overrides it — so `CM 020` isn't matched against `CMD2`.
  CommandErrorFormatter.cs      # Pure formatter for unrecognized-command errors: when the leading token is a known callsign (partial/complete), names the verb after it instead of blaming the callsign. Used by MainViewModel.SendCommandAsync.
  MeasureEndpointResolver.cs    # Pure resolver for `.rbl A B` / `*T A B` endpoint tokens: exact callsign → fix/FRD → partial callsign, with ambiguity + navdata-not-ready errors. Used by MainViewModel's measure dot-command.
  LayoutService.cs              # Captures/applies named layouts (SavedLayout in UserPreferences): per-window geometry + the six pop-out toggles + the docked terminal's size + extra Radar/Ground windows + favorites state (loaded sets and their bar order) + open Strips/vTDLS tabs + DataGrid column layout; never view settings. Surfaced via View → Layout ▸; a partial apply (Apply Layout dialog) stages only the ticked groups and leaves favorite sets alone.
  ViewSettingsCopyCatalog.cs    # Shared catalog of the Apply Layout dialog's groups: Ground/Radar per-scenario view settings (scenario source) and LayoutGroups (saved-layout source), each Key/Label/Describe/AreEqual/Copy. Single source of truth for the dialog's diff rows and MainWindow's merge-on-apply.
  ShownRouteBuilder.cs          # Pure builder for the radar "Show nav route" overlay. Produces a multi-segment path from AircraftModel.NavRouteFixes (server-provided positions, so arcs/custom/FRD fixes draw verbatim; synthetic arc vertices carry empty names) with per-fix crossing-restriction labels, plus a procedure vector tail (5 nm arrow off the last STAR fix on FM/VM/VA legs) + the expected approach line (IAF/transition → FAF → threshold, FAC extended back 5 nm when no transition is named).
  UiThreadWatchdog.cs           # Background thread that logs a [warn] + runtime/memory snapshot (working set, managed heap, .NET memory-load %, last GC pause, thread count) when the Avalonia dispatcher stalls >2s, and the stall duration + GC deltas on recovery. Diagnoses otherwise-traceless UI-thread freezes; started from App.OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted (desktop lifetime only). Past 15s it also shows the user a native (non-Avalonia, since the dispatcher is wedged) message box pointing at the log — once per process, Windows only. On Windows a hard freeze also writes a thread-info minidump via FreezeDumpWriter and logs every thread's managed call stack (UI thread first) via ManagedStackCapture.
  ManagedStackCapture.cs       # ClrMD snapshot self-attach (Windows-only) that formats every live thread's managed stack, requested (UI) thread first. Called once by UiThreadWatchdog on a 15s hard freeze so a wedged dispatcher is diagnosable from the log (GitHub #347).
  FreezeDumpWriter.cs          # MiniDumpWriteDump self-capture (Windows-only): writes yaat-freeze-<timestamp>.dmp (thread-info flags only — a few MB, no heap/user data) next to yaat-client.log on a 15s hard freeze, keeping the newest 3. Carries the native stacks ManagedStackCapture cannot walk (GitHub #347's wedge was below a native transition).
  Discord/                      # Discord Rich Presence over Discord's local RPC-over-IPC channel, hand-rolled with no package dependency — see docs/discord-integration.md "Desktop client Rich Presence"
    IRichPresencePublisher.cs   # What MainViewModel sees: Publish(DiscordActivity) / Clear(), fire-and-forget, never throw
    DiscordActivity.cs          # DiscordActivity record (Details, State, StartUnixSeconds) + DiscordRpcJson: Utf8JsonWriter builders for the handshake and SET_ACTIVITY payloads, text fields clamped to 128 chars
